Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getLeastSupertype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 867-873
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
867 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
868 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
869 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
870 |       return that.toMaybeUnionType().getLeastSupertype(this);
871 |     }
872 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
873 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.declareNameInScope(FlowScope,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 158-181
Documentation:
/**
   * Declares a refined type in {@code scope} for the name represented by
   * {@code node}. It must be possible to refine the type of the given node in
   * the given scope, as determined by {@link #getTypeIfRefinable}.
   */
Method code:
158 |   protected void declareNameInScope(FlowScope scope, Node node, JSType type) {
159 |     switch (node.getType()) {
160 |       case Token.NAME:
161 |         scope.inferSlotType(node.getString(), type);
162 |         break;
163 | 
164 |       case Token.GETPROP:
165 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
166 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(qualifiedName);
167 | 
168 |         JSType origType = node.getJSType();
169 |         origType = origType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : origType;
170 |         scope.inferQualifiedSlot(node, qualifiedName, origType, type);
171 |         break;
172 | 
173 |       case Token.THIS:
174 |         // "this" references aren't currently modeled in the CFG.
175 |         break;
176 | 
177 |       default:
178 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("Node cannot be refined. \n" +
179 |             node.toStringTree());
180 |     }
181 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(JSType,String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 672-684
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a version of {@code type} that is restricted by some knowledge
   * about the result of the {@code typeof} operation.
   * <p>
   * The behavior of the {@code typeof} operator can be summarized by the
   * following table:
   * <table>
   * <tr><th>type</th><th>result</th></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code undefined}</td><td>"undefined"</td></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code null}</td><td>"object"</td></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code boolean}</td><td>"boolean"</td></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code number}</td><td>"number"</td></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code string}</td><td>"string"</td></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code Object} (which doesn't implement [[Call]])</td>
   *     <td>"object"</td></tr>
   * <tr><td>{@code Object} (which implements [[Call]])</td>
   *     <td>"function"</td></tr>
   * </table>
   * @param type the type to restrict
   * @param value A value known to be equal or not equal to the result of the
   *        {@code typeof} operation
   * @param resultEqualsValue {@code true} if the {@code typeOf} result is known
   *        to equal {@code value}; {@code false} if it is known <em>not</em> to
   *        equal {@code value}
   * @return the restricted type or null if no version of the type matches the
   *         restriction
   */
Method code:
672 |   JSType getRestrictedByTypeOfResult(JSType type, String value,
673 |                                      boolean resultEqualsValue) {
674 |     if (type == null) {
675 |       if (resultEqualsValue) {
676 |         JSType result = getNativeTypeForTypeOf(value);
677 |         return result == null ? getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE) : result;
678 |       } else {
679 |         return null;
680 |       }
681 |     }
682 |     return type.visit(
683 |         new RestrictByOneTypeOfResultVisitor(value, resultEqualsValue));
684 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */
Method code:
313 |   public FunctionType toMaybeFunctionType() {

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addOptionalParams(JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 79-88
Documentation:
/**
   * Add optional parameters of the given type to the end of the param list.
   * @param types Types for each optional parameter. The builder will make them
   *     undefine-able.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
79 |   public boolean addOptionalParams(JSType ...types) {
80 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
81 |       return false;
82 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1247 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1248 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1249 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1250 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1251 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1252 |       } else {
1253 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1254 |       }
1255 |     }
1256 |     return builder.build();
1257 |   }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.toStringHelper(boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 927-967
Documentation:
/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
927 |   @Override
928 |   String toStringHelper(boolean forAnnotations) {
929 |     if (!isPrettyPrint() ||
930 |         this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
931 |       return "Function";
932 |     }
933 | 
934 |     setPrettyPrint(false);
935 | 
936 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
937 |     b.append("function (");
938 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
939 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !(typeOfThis instanceof UnknownType);
940 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
941 |       if (isConstructor()) {
942 |         b.append("new:");
943 |       } else {
944 |         b.append("this:");
945 |       }
946 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
947 |     }
948 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
949 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
950 |         b.append(", ");
951 |       }
952 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
953 |       appendArgString(b, p, forAnnotations);
954 | 
955 |       p = p.getNext();
956 |       while (p != null) {
957 |         b.append(", ");
958 |         appendArgString(b, p, forAnnotations);
959 |         p = p.getNext();
960 |       }
961 |     }
962 |     b.append("): ");
963 |     b.append(call.returnType.toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
964 | 
965 |     setPrettyPrint(true);
966 |     return b.toString();
967 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1185-1213
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1185 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1186 |     // unknown
1187 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1188 |       return true;
1189 |     }
1190 |     // all type
1191 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1192 |       return true;
1193 |     }
1194 |     // equality
1195 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1196 |       return true;
1197 |     }
1198 |     // unions
1199 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1200 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1201 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1202 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1203 |           return true;
1204 |         }
1205 |       }
1206 |     }
1207 |     // proxy types
1208 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1209 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1210 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1211 |     }
1212 |     return false;
1213 |   }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 498-518
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
498 |   @Override
499 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
500 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
501 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
502 |     if (unknown) {
503 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
504 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
505 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
506 |         unknown = false;
507 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
508 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
509 |             unknown = true;
510 |             break;
511 |           }
512 |         }
513 |       } else {
514 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
515 |       }
516 |     }
517 |     return unknown;
518 |   }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(UnionType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionType.java
Original range: 333-344
Documentation:
/**
   * Two union types are equal if they have the same number of alternates
   * and all alternates are equal.
   */
Method code:
333 |   boolean checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
334 |       UnionType that, boolean tolerateUnknowns) {
335 |     if (!tolerateUnknowns && alternates.size() != that.alternates.size()) {
336 |       return false;
337 |     }
338 |     for (JSType alternate : that.alternates) {
339 |       if (!hasAlternate(alternate, tolerateUnknowns)) {
340 |         return false;
341 |       }
342 |     }
343 |     return true;
344 |   }

Method code:
376 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
377 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
378 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
379 |       return position;
380 |     } else {
381 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
382 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
383 |     }
384 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 83-105
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if a variable is declared.
   */
Method code:
552 |   public boolean isDeclared(String name, boolean recurse) {
553 |     Scope scope = this;
554 |     if (scope.vars.containsKey(name))
555 |       return true;
556 | 
557 |     if (scope.parent != null && recurse) {
558 |       return scope.parent.isDeclared(name, recurse);
559 |     }
560 |     return false;
561 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 158-174
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
158 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
159 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
160 | 
161 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
162 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
163 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
164 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
165 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
166 |       }
167 | 
168 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
169 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
170 |       if (first.isName() &&
171 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
172 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
173 |       }
174 |     }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.defineProperty(String,JSType,boolean,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 233-249
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   @Override
234 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
235 |       Node propertyNode) {
236 |     if (hasOwnDeclaredProperty(name)) {
237 |       return false;
238 |     }
239 |     Property newProp = new Property(
240 |         name, type, inferred, propertyNode);
241 |     Property oldProp = properties.get(name);
242 |     if (oldProp != null) {
243 |       // This is to keep previously inferred JsDoc info, e.g., in a
244 |       // replaceScript scenario.
245 |       newProp.setJSDocInfo(oldProp.getJSDocInfo());
246 |     }
247 |     properties.put(name, newProp);
248 |     return true;
249 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.getPropertiesCount()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 151-164
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the number of properties of this object.
   */
Method code:
151 |   @Override
152 |   public int getPropertiesCount() {
153 |     ObjectType implicitPrototype = getImplicitPrototype();
154 |     if (implicitPrototype == null) {
155 |       return this.properties.size();
156 |     }
157 |     int localCount = 0;
158 |     for (String property : properties.keySet()) {
159 |       if (!implicitPrototype.hasProperty(property)) {
160 |         localCount++;
161 |       }
162 |     }
163 |     return implicitPrototype.getPropertiesCount() + localCount;
164 |   }

===== M0189 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 403-429
Documentation:

Method code:
403 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
404 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
405 |     // two cases:
406 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
407 |     // and
408 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
409 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
410 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
411 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
412 |     //
413 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
414 |     //
415 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
416 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
417 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
418 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
419 |     //
420 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
421 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
422 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
423 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
424 | 
425 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
426 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
427 |     }
428 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
429 |   }

===== M0211 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 962-988
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infima, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
962 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
963 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
964 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
965 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
966 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
967 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
968 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
969 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
970 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
971 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
972 |           needsFiltering = true;
973 |           break;
974 |         }
975 |       }
976 | 
977 |       if (needsFiltering) {
978 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
979 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
980 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
981 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
982 |           }
983 |         }
984 |         return builder.build();
985 |       }
986 |     }
987 |     return type;
988 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 274-337
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
282 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
283 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
284 |         setErrorManager(printer);
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;
291 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
294 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
295 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
296 |       // parser type warnings.
297 |       options.setWarningLevel(
298 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
299 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 510-591
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
510 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, boolean tolerateUnknowns) {
511 |     if (this == that) {
512 |       return true;
513 |     }
514 | 
515 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
516 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
517 |     if (isUnknownType() || that.isUnknownType()) {
518 |       if (tolerateUnknowns) {
519 |         // If we tolerate unknowns, then two types are the same if they're
520 |         // both unknown.
521 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
522 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
523 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
524 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
525 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
526 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
527 |         return false;
528 |       }
529 |     }
530 | 
531 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
532 |       return this.toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
533 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), tolerateUnknowns);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
537 |       return this.toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
538 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), tolerateUnknowns);
539 |     }
540 | 
541 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
542 |       return this.toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
543 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), tolerateUnknowns);
544 |     }
545 | 
546 |     ParameterizedType thisParamType = toMaybeParameterizedType();
547 |     ParameterizedType thatParamType = that.toMaybeParameterizedType();
548 |     if (thisParamType != null || thatParamType != null) {
549 |       // Check if one type is parameterized, but the other is not.
550 |       boolean paramsMatch = false;
551 |       if (thisParamType != null && thatParamType != null) {
552 |         paramsMatch = thisParamType.getParameterType().checkEquivalenceHelper(
553 |             thatParamType.getParameterType(), tolerateUnknowns);
554 |       } else if (tolerateUnknowns) {
555 |         paramsMatch = true;
556 |       } else {
557 |         paramsMatch = false;
558 |       }
559 | 
560 |       JSType thisRootType = thisParamType == null ?
561 |           this : thisParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
562 |       JSType thatRootType = thatParamType == null ?
563 |           that : thatParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
564 |       return paramsMatch &&
565 |           thisRootType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatRootType, tolerateUnknowns);
566 |     }
567 | 
568 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
569 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
570 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
571 |     }
572 | 
573 |     // Unbox other proxies.
574 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
575 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
576 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
577 |               that, tolerateUnknowns);
578 |     }
579 | 
580 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
581 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
582 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
583 |           tolerateUnknowns);
584 |     }
585 | 
586 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
587 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
588 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
589 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
590 |     return this == that;
591 |   }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-490
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 | 
467 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
474 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
475 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
476 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
477 |           return true;
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     // other prototype based objects
483 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
484 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
485 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
486 |       // to avoid guessing.
487 |       return true;
488 |     }
489 |     return thatObj != null && this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
490 |   }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getSuperClassConstructor()

/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, get its superclass constructor
   * or {@code null} if none exists.
   */
Method code:
834 |   public FunctionType getSuperClassConstructor() {
835 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(isConstructor() || isInterface());

837 |     if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {

===== M0244 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 448-483
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
448 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
449 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
450 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
451 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
452 | 
453 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
454 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
455 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
456 | 
457 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
458 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
459 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
460 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
464 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
465 |       if (superClass != null) {
466 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (isInterface()) {
470 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
471 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
472 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
473 |           }
474 |         }
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
479 |       clearCachedValues();
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     return true;
483 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
478 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {

480 |          current != null;

482 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {

484 |       }
485 |     }
486 |     return false;
487 |   }

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 409-422
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
409 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
410 |       Node ret;
411 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
412 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
413 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
414 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
415 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
416 |       } else {
417 |         ret = transform(n);
418 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
419 |       }
420 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
421 |       return ret;
422 |     }

===== M0282 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.getTypeIfRefinable(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 119-151
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns the type of a node in the given scope if the node corresponds to a
   * name whose type is capable of being refined.
   * @return The current type of the node if it can be refined, null otherwise.
   */
Method code:
119 |   protected JSType getTypeIfRefinable(Node node, FlowScope scope) {
120 |     switch (node.getType()) {
121 |       case Token.NAME:
122 |         StaticSlot<JSType> nameVar = scope.getSlot(node.getString());
123 |         if (nameVar != null) {
124 |           JSType nameVarType = nameVar.getType();
125 |           if (nameVarType == null) {
126 |             nameVarType = node.getJSType();
127 |           }
128 |           return nameVarType;
129 |         }
130 |         return null;
131 | 
132 |       case Token.GETPROP:
133 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
134 |         if (qualifiedName == null) {
135 |           return null;
136 |         }
137 |         StaticSlot<JSType> propVar = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
138 |         JSType propVarType = null;
139 |         if (propVar != null) {
140 |           propVarType = propVar.getType();
141 |         }
142 |         if (propVarType == null) {
143 |           propVarType = node.getJSType();
144 |         }
145 |         if (propVarType == null) {
146 |           propVarType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
147 |         }
148 |         return propVarType;
149 |     }
150 |     return null;
151 |   }

===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 785-798
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;
788 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
789 |       return item.getNext();
790 |     } else {
791 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
792 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
793 |         return item.chain(result);
794 |       } else {
795 |         return item;
796 |       }
797 |     }
798 |   }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1177-1179
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1177 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1178 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1179 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */

65 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {

68 |   }

87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {

   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
1008 |   @Override
1009 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1010 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
1011 |       return true;
1012 |     }
1013 | 
1014 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
1015 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
1016 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
1017 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
1018 |         return true;
1019 |       }
1020 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
1021 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
1025 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
1026 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
1027 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
1028 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
1029 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
1030 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
1031 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
1032 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
1033 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
1034 | 
1035 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
1036 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
1037 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
1038 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
1039 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
1040 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
1041 | 
1042 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
1043 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
1044 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
1045 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
1046 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
1047 |     }
1048 | 
1049 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
1050 |   }

Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1176-1184
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1177 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1178 |       return -1;
1179 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1180 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1181 |     } else {
1182 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1183 |     }
1184 |   }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.maybeRestrictName(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 392-400
Documentation:
/**
   * If the restrictedType differs from the originalType, then we should
   * branch the current flow scope and create a new flow scope with the name
   * declared with the new type.
   *
   * We try not to create spurious child flow scopes as this makes type
   * inference slower.
   *
   * We also do not want spurious slots around in type inference, because
   * we use these as a signal for "checked unknown" types. A "checked unknown"
   * type is a symbol that the programmer has already checked and verified that
   * it's defined, even if we don't know what it is.
   *
   * It is OK to pass non-name nodes into this method, as long as you pass
   * in {@code null} for a restricted type.
   */
Method code:
392 |   private FlowScope maybeRestrictName(
393 |       FlowScope blindScope, Node node, JSType originalType, JSType restrictedType) {
394 |     if (restrictedType != null && restrictedType != originalType) {
395 |       FlowScope informed = blindScope.createChildFlowScope();
396 |       declareNameInScope(informed, node, restrictedType);
397 |       return informed;
398 |     }
399 |     return blindScope;
400 |   }

===== M0431 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 124-264
Documentation:

Method code:
124 |   @Override
125 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
126 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
127 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
128 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
129 |     switch (operatorToken) {
130 |       case Token.EQ:
131 |       case Token.NE:
132 |       case Token.SHEQ:
133 |       case Token.SHNE:
134 |       case Token.CASE:
135 |         Node left;
136 |         Node right;
137 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
138 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
139 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
140 |         } else {
141 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
142 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
146 |         Node stringNode = null;
147 |         if (left.isTypeOf() && right.isString()) {
148 |           typeOfNode = left;
149 |           stringNode = right;
150 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() &&
151 |                    left.isString()) {
152 |           typeOfNode = right;
153 |           stringNode = left;
154 |         }
155 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
156 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
157 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
158 |           if (operandType != null) {
159 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
160 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
161 |             if (!outcome) {
162 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
163 |             }
164 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
165 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
166 |           }
167 |         }
168 |     }
169 |     switch (operatorToken) {
170 |       case Token.AND:
171 |         if (outcome) {
172 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
173 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
174 |         } else {
175 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
176 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
177 |         }
178 | 
179 |       case Token.OR:
180 |         if (!outcome) {
181 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
182 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
183 |         } else {
184 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
185 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
186 |         }
187 | 
188 |       case Token.EQ:
189 |         if (outcome) {
190 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
191 |         } else {
192 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
193 |         }
194 | 
195 |       case Token.NE:
196 |         if (outcome) {
197 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
198 |         } else {
199 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
200 |         }
201 | 
202 |       case Token.SHEQ:
203 |         if (outcome) {
204 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
205 |         } else {
206 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
207 |         }
208 | 
209 |       case Token.SHNE:
210 |         if (outcome) {
211 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
212 |         } else {
213 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
214 |         }
215 | 
216 |       case Token.NAME:
217 |       case Token.GETPROP:
218 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
219 | 
220 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
221 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
222 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
223 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
224 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
225 |             outcome);
226 | 
227 |       case Token.NOT:
228 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
229 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
230 | 
231 |       case Token.LE:
232 |       case Token.LT:
233 |       case Token.GE:
234 |       case Token.GT:
235 |         if (outcome) {
236 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, INEQ);
237 |         }
238 |         break;
239 | 
240 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
241 |         return caseInstanceOf(
242 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
243 |             outcome);
244 | 
245 |       case Token.IN:
246 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().isString()) {
247 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
248 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
249 |         }
250 |         break;
251 | 
252 |       case Token.CASE:
253 |         Node left =
254 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
255 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
256 |         if (outcome) {
257 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
258 |         } else {
259 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
260 |         }
261 |     }
262 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
263 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
264 |   }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

201 |   private void setFileOverviewJsDoc(Node irNode) {
202 |     // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
203 |     // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
204 |     // to that node.
205 |     JSDocInfo rootNodeJsDoc = rootNodeJsDocHolder.getJSDocInfo();
206 |     if (rootNodeJsDoc != null) {
207 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(rootNodeJsDoc);
208 |       rootNodeJsDoc.setAssociatedNode(irNode);

212 |       if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
213 |           (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
214 |         fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
215 |       }
216 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);

218 |     }
